Motivation: 结肠息肉是结直肠癌的前体病变，但其外观、位置和大小的高度变异性导致结肠镜检查中漏检和不完全切除率高，严重影响患者预后。现有自动化检测方法虽然有所改进，但缺乏对息肉生长动态变化和时序关系的考虑。

Method: 研究采用多中心、多样化人群的综合性数据集，开发基于深度学习的实时检测与分割方法，特别强调利用序列数据和帧间时序关系来捕捉息肉的动态变化特征。

Result: 研究表明，整合序列数据和时序信息能显著提升息肉检测与分割的精确度，通过捕捉息肉随时间变化的动态特征，有助于降低漏检率并提高诊断准确性。

Conclusion: 在结肠息肉自动化检测与分割方法中融入序列数据和时序信息至关重要，这能更好地模拟临床实际场景中的动态变化，为实时临床应用提供更可靠的诊断支持，最终改善患者管理效果。

Abstract: Colonic polyps are well-recognized precursors to colorectal cancer (CRC), typically detected during colonoscopy. However, the variability in appearance, location, and size of these polyps complicates their detection and removal, leading to challenges in effective surveillance, intervention, and subsequently CRC prevention. The processes of colonoscopy surveillance and polyp removal are highly reliant on the expertise of gastroenterologists and occur within the complexities of the colonic structure. As a result, there is a high rate of missed detections and incomplete removal of colonic polyps, which can adversely impact patient outcomes. Recently, automated methods that use machine learning have been developed to enhance polyps detection and segmentation, thus helping clinical processes and reducing missed rates. These advancements highlight the potential for improving diagnostic accuracy in real-time applications, which ultimately facilitates more effective patient management. Furthermore, integrating sequence data and temporal information could significantly enhance the precision of these methods by capturing the dynamic nature of polyp growth and the changes that occur over time. To rigorously investigate these challenges, data scientists and experts gastroenterologists collaborated to compile a comprehensive dataset that spans multiple centers and diverse populations. This initiative aims to underscore the critical importance of incorporating sequence data and temporal information in the development of robust automated detection and segmentation methods. This study evaluates the applicability of deep learning techniques developed in real-time clinical colonoscopy tasks using sequence data, highlighting the critical role of temporal relationships between frames in improving diagnostic precision.

</details>


### [75] [Gaussian Wardrobe: Compositional 3D Gaussian Avatars for Free-Form Virtual Try-On](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04290)
*Zhiyi Chen,Hsuan-I Ho,Tianjian Jiang,Jie Song,Manuel Kaufmann,Chen Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Gaussian Wardrobe：一种从多视角视频中数字化组合式3D神经化身的新框架，将人体与服装分离为可组合的层次结构


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D神经化身方法通常将人体和服装视为不可分割的整体，这种范式无法捕捉复杂自由形态服装的动态特性，且限制了服装在不同个体间的复用

Method: 开发了一种新颖的组合式3D高斯表示，从多层自由形态服装构建化身；核心是将神经化身分解为身体和形状无关的神经服装层，从多视角视频中解耦每层服装并将其规范化到形状独立空间

Result: 方法能够建模具有高保真动态的逼真化身，在新姿态合成基准测试中达到新的最先进性能；学习的组合式服装构成了多功能数字衣橱，实现了服装可自由转移到新主体的实用虚拟试穿应用

Conclusion: Gaussian Wardrobe框架成功解决了现有方法在服装动态建模和复用方面的限制，为数字化身创建提供了更灵活、可组合的解决方案

Abstract: We introduce Gaussian Wardrobe, a novel framework to digitalize compositional 3D neural avatars from multi-view videos. Existing methods for 3D neural avatars typically treat the human body and clothing as an inseparable entity. However, this paradigm fails to capture the dynamics of complex free-form garments and limits the reuse of clothing across different individuals. To overcome these problems, we develop a novel, compositional 3D Gaussian representation to build avatars from multiple layers of free-form garments. The core of our method is decomposing neural avatars into bodies and layers of shape-agnostic neural garments. To achieve this, our framework learns to disentangle each garment layer from multi-view videos and canonicalizes it into a shape-independent space. In experiments, our method models photorealistic avatars with high-fidelity dynamics, achieving new state-of-the-art performance on novel pose synthesis benchmarks. In addition, we demonstrate that the learned compositional garments contribute to a versatile digital wardrobe, enabling a practical virtual try-on application where clothing can be freely transferred to new subjects. Project page: https://ait.ethz.ch/gaussianwardrobe

</details>


### [76] [CubeComposer: Spatio-Temporal Autoregressive 4K 360° Video Generation from Perspective Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04291)
*Lingen Li,Guangzhi Wang,Xiaoyu Li,Zhaoyang Zhang,Qi Dou,Jinwei Gu,Tianfan Xue,Ying Shan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CubeComposer是一个新颖的时空自回归扩散模型，能够原生生成4K分辨率的360°全景视频，通过立方体贴图表示和创新的自回归策略解决高分辨率生成的内存限制问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法受限于普通扩散模型的计算限制，只能支持≤1K分辨率原生生成，并依赖次优的后处理超分辨率来提高分辨率。高质量的4K 360°全景视频对VR沉浸体验至关重要，需要解决高分辨率原生生成的技术挑战。

Method: 1) 将视频分解为六个面的立方体贴图表示；2) 采用时空自回归策略，在立方体面和时间窗口上有序合成内容；3) 设计立方体面上下文管理机制，包含稀疏上下文注意力提高效率；4) 提出连续性感知技术，包括立方体感知位置编码、填充和混合以消除边界接缝。

Result: 在基准数据集上的大量实验表明，CubeComposer在原生分辨率和视觉质量方面优于最先进的方法，支持实际的VR应用场景。

Conclusion: CubeComposer通过创新的立方体贴图表示和时空自回归策略，成功实现了4K分辨率360°视频的原生生成，解决了现有方法的内存限制问题，为高质量VR内容创建提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Generating high-quality 360° panoramic videos from perspective input is one of the crucial applications for virtual reality (VR), whereby high-resolution videos are especially important for immersive experience. Existing methods are constrained by computational limitations of vanilla diffusion models, only supporting $\leq$ 1K resolution native generation and relying on suboptimal post super-resolution to increase resolution. We introduce CubeComposer, a novel spatio-temporal autoregressive diffusion model that natively generates 4K-resolution 360° videos. By decomposing videos into cubemap representations with six faces, CubeComposer autoregressively synthesizes content in a well-planned spatio-temporal order, reducing memory demands while enabling high-resolution output. Specifically, to address challenges in multi-dimensional autoregression, we propose: (1) a spatio-temporal autoregressive strategy that orchestrates 360° video generation across cube faces and time windows for coherent synthesis; (2) a cube face context management mechanism, equipped with a sparse context attention design to improve efficiency; and (3) continuity-aware techniques, including cube-aware positional encoding, padding, and blending to eliminate boundary seams. Extensive experiments on benchmark datasets demonstrate that CubeComposer outperforms state-of-the-art methods in native resolution and visual quality, supporting practical VR application scenarios. Project page: https://lg-li.github.io/project/cubecomposer

</details>


### [77] [Motion Manipulation via Unsupervised Keypoint Positioning in Face Animation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04302)
*Hong Li,Boyu Liu,Xuhui Liu,Baochang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MMFA提出了一种基于无监督关键点定位的面部动画新方法，通过自监督表示学习和变分自编码器实现身份语义与运动信息的解耦，从而实现可控的面部运动生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于无监督关键点定位的面部动画方法虽然能生成逼真的虚拟肖像，但无法实现可控的面部生成，因为现有的关键点分解流程未能完全解耦身份语义和交织的运动信息（如旋转、平移和表情）。

Method: 1. 引入自监督表示学习来编码和解码潜在特征空间中的表情，并将其与其他运动信息解耦；2. 提出新的关键点计算方法以实现任意运动控制；3. 设计变分自编码器将表情特征映射到连续高斯分布，首次在无监督框架中实现表情插值。

Result: 在公开数据集上的大量实验验证了MMFA的有效性，结果显示MMFA在创建逼真动画和操纵面部运动方面相比先前方法具有显著优势。

Conclusion: MMFA通过创新的无监督关键点定位方法成功解决了面部动画中身份与运动信息解耦的挑战，实现了更可控、更逼真的面部运动生成和操纵。

Abstract: Face animation deals with controlling and generating facial features with a wide range of applications. The methods based on unsupervised keypoint positioning can produce realistic and detailed virtual portraits. However, they cannot achieve controllable face generation since the existing keypoint decomposition pipelines fail to fully decouple identity semantics and intertwined motion information (e.g., rotation, translation, and expression). To address these issues, we present a new method, Motion Manipulation via unsupervised keypoint positioning in Face Animation (MMFA). We first introduce self-supervised representation learning to encode and decode expressions in the latent feature space and decouple them from other motion information. Secondly, we propose a new way to compute keypoints aiming to achieve arbitrary motion control. Moreover, we design a variational autoencoder to map expression features to a continuous Gaussian distribution, allowing us for the first time to interpolate facial expressions in an unsupervised framework. We have conducted extensive experiments on publicly available datasets to validate the effectiveness of MMFA, which show that MMFA offers pronounced advantages over prior arts in creating realistic animation and manipulating face motion.

</details>


### [78] [Dual Diffusion Models for Multi-modal Guided 3D Avatar Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04307)
*Hong Li,Yutang Feng,Minqi Meng,Yichen Yang,Xuhui Liu,Baochang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PromptAvatar：一种基于双扩散模型的高效3D头像生成框架，通过多模态数据集和直接映射方法，在10秒内生成高质量、无阴影的3D头像，显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本驱动方法依赖迭代优化，存在语义控制不精细和推理速度慢的问题；图像驱动方法受限于高质量3D面部扫描数据的稀缺和高成本，限制了模型泛化能力。

Method: 首先构建包含10万对多模态数据（细粒度文本描述、野外人脸图像、高质量光照归一化纹理UV贴图、3D几何形状）的数据集；提出PromptAvatar框架，包含纹理扩散模型（支持文本/图像多条件引导）和几何扩散模型（文本引导），学习从多模态提示到3D表示的直接映射。

Result: 无需耗时迭代优化，在10秒内生成高质量、无阴影的3D头像；在生成质量、细粒度细节对齐和计算效率方面显著优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: PromptAvatar通过大规模多模态数据集和双扩散模型架构，成功解决了现有方法在语义控制、推理速度和数据依赖方面的限制，实现了高效高质量的3D头像生成。

Abstract: Generating high-fidelity 3D avatars from text or image prompts is highly sought after in virtual reality and human-computer interaction. However, existing text-driven methods often rely on iterative Score Distillation Sampling (SDS) or CLIP optimization, which struggle with fine-grained semantic control and suffer from excessively slow inference. Meanwhile, image-driven approaches are severely bottlenecked by the scarcity and high acquisition cost of high-quality 3D facial scans, limiting model generalization. To address these challenges, we first construct a novel, large-scale dataset comprising over 100,000 pairs across four modalities: fine-grained textual descriptions, in-the-wild face images, high-quality light-normalized texture UV maps, and 3D geometric shapes. Leveraging this comprehensive dataset, we propose PromptAvatar, a framework featuring dual diffusion models. Specifically, it integrates a Texture Diffusion Model (TDM) that supports flexible multi-condition guidance from text and/or image prompts, alongside a Geometry Diffusion Model (GDM) guided by text prompts. By learning the direct mapping from multi-modal prompts to 3D representations, PromptAvatar eliminates the need for time-consuming iterative optimization, successfully generating high-fidelity, shading-free 3D avatars in under 10 seconds. Extensive quantitative and qualitative experiments demonstrate that our method significantly outperforms existing state-of-the-art approaches in generation quality, fine-grained detail alignment, and computational efficiency.

</details>


### [79] [MOO: A Multi-view Oriented Observations Dataset for Viewpoint Analysis in Cattle Re-Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04314)
*William Grolleau,Achraf Chaouch,Astrid Sabourin,Guillaume Lapouge,Catherine Achard*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出了MOO数据集，一个包含1000头牛从128个均匀采样视角拍摄的大规模合成空中-地面动物重识别数据集，用于系统分析视角变化对模型性能的影响，并发现了一个关键的仰角阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动物重识别在视角变化下面临挑战，特别是在空中-地面场景中，现有数据集缺乏精确的角度标注来系统分析几何变化对模型性能的影响。

Method: 构建MOO合成数据集，包含1000头牛从128个均匀采样视角的128,000张标注图像，使用该数据集量化仰角影响，识别关键仰角阈值，并在真实世界数据集上验证迁移能力。

Result: 发现存在一个关键的仰角阈值，超过该阈值后模型对未见视角的泛化能力显著提升；在四个真实世界牛数据集上的零样本和监督设置中均获得性能提升，证实合成几何先验能有效弥合领域差距。

Conclusion: MOO数据集和分析为跨视角动物重识别模型的未来发展奠定了基础，合成几何先验能有效提升模型在真实世界应用中的性能。

Abstract: Animal re-identification (ReID) faces critical challenges due to viewpoint variations, particularly in Aerial-Ground (AG-ReID) settings where models must match individuals across drastic elevation changes. However, existing datasets lack the precise angular annotations required to systematically analyze these geometric variations. To address this, we introduce the Multi-view Oriented Observation (MOO) dataset, a large-scale synthetic AG-ReID dataset of $1,000$ cattle individuals captured from $128$ uniformly sampled viewpoints ($128,000$ annotated images). Using this controlled dataset, we quantify the influence of elevation and identify a critical elevation threshold, above which models generalize significantly better to unseen views. Finally, we validate the transferability to real-world applications in both zero-shot and supervised settings, demonstrating performance gains across four real-world cattle datasets and confirming that synthetic geometric priors effectively bridge the domain gap. Collectively, this dataset and analysis lay the foundation for future model development in cross-view animal ReID. MOO is publicly available at https://github.com/TurtleSmoke/MOO.

</details>


### [80] [Scalable Evaluation of the Realism of Synthetic Environmental Augmentations in Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04325)
*Damian J. Ruck,Paul Vautravers,Oliver Chalkley,Jake Thomas*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一个评估合成图像编辑方法真实性的可扩展框架，应用于汽车摄像头图像添加环境条件（雾、雨、雪、夜间），发现生成式AI方法显著优于基于规则的方法，最佳生成方法达到最佳规则方法约3.6倍的接受率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI系统评估需要合成测试用例，特别是罕见或安全关键条件难以在操作数据中观察到。生成式AI通过可控图像编辑提供了有前景的方法，但其有效性取决于生成的图像是否足够真实以支持有意义的评估。

Method: 提出可扩展框架评估合成图像编辑方法的真实性，应用于汽车摄像头图像添加环境条件（雾、雨、雪、夜间）。使用40张晴天图像，比较基于规则的增强库与生成式AI图像编辑模型。使用两种互补的自动化指标评估真实性：视觉语言模型（VLM）陪审团进行感知真实性评估，以及基于嵌入的分布分析测量与真实恶劣条件图像的相似性。

Result: 生成式AI方法显著优于基于规则的方法，最佳生成方法达到最佳规则方法约3.6倍的接受率。性能因条件而异：雾最容易模拟，而夜间变换仍然具有挑战性。值得注意的是，VLM陪审团对真实恶劣条件图像也分配了不完美的接受率，为合成方法建立了实际的上限标准。按照这一标准，领先的生成方法在大多数条件下匹配或超过了真实图像的性能。

Conclusion: 现代生成式图像编辑模型能够为评估流程实现可扩展的生成真实恶劣条件图像。该框架提供了可扩展真实性评估的实用方法，但未来工作仍需验证与人类研究的一致性。

Abstract: Evaluation of AI systems often requires synthetic test cases, particularly for rare or safety-critical conditions that are difficult to observe in operational data. Generative AI offers a promising approach for producing such data through controllable image editing, but its usefulness depends on whether the resulting images are sufficiently realistic to support meaningful evaluation.
  We present a scalable framework for assessing the realism of synthetic image-editing methods and apply it to the task of adding environmental conditions-fog, rain, snow, and nighttime-to car-mounted camera images. Using 40 clear-day images, we compare rule-based augmentation libraries with generative AI image-editing models. Realism is evaluated using two complementary automated metrics: a vision-language model (VLM) jury for perceptual realism assessment, and embedding-based distributional analysis to measure similarity to genuine adverse-condition imagery.
  Generative AI methods substantially outperform rule-based approaches, with the best generative method achieving approximately 3.6 times the acceptance rate of the best rule-based method. Performance varies across conditions: fog proves easiest to simulate, while nighttime transformations remain challenging. Notably, the VLM jury assigns imperfect acceptance even to real adverse-condition imagery, establishing practical ceilings against which synthetic methods can be judged. By this standard, leading generative methods match or exceed real-image performance for most conditions.
  These results suggest that modern generative image-editing models can enable scalable generation of realistic adverse-condition imagery for evaluation pipelines. Our framework therefore provides a practical approach for scalable realism evaluation, though validation against human studies remains an important direction for future work.

</details>


### [81] [Pointer-CAD: Unifying B-Rep and Command Sequences via Pointer-based Edges & Faces Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04337)
*Dacheng Qi,Chenyu Wang,Jingwei Xu,Tianzhe Chu,Zibo Zhao,Wen Liu,Wenrui Ding,Yi Ma,Shenghua Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Pointer-CAD：基于指针的LLM-CAD生成框架，通过指针选择几何实体并减少量化误差，显著提升复杂结构生成能力


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的CAD生成方法使用命令序列表示，但存在两个主要问题：1) 不支持实体选择（如面或边），限制了倒角、圆角等复杂编辑操作；2) 草图和拉伸操作中连续变量的离散化导致拓扑错误

Method: 提出Pointer-CAD框架：1) 使用基于指针的命令序列表示，将B-rep模型的几何信息显式纳入序列建模；2) 将CAD生成分解为步骤，每个步骤基于文本描述和前一步生成的B-rep进行条件生成；3) 当操作需要选择特定几何实体时，LLM预测指针从可用集合中选择特征最一致的候选；4) 开发数据标注流程构建约57.5万个CAD模型的数据集

Result: 实验结果表明：Pointer-CAD有效支持复杂几何结构生成，将分割误差降至极低水平，相比先前命令序列方法有显著改进，显著减轻了量化误差引入的拓扑不准确性

Conclusion: Pointer-CAD通过指针机制解决了传统LLM-CAD方法在实体选择和量化误差方面的限制，为基于LLM的CAD生成提供了更实用、准确的解决方案

Abstract: Constructing computer-aided design (CAD) models is labor-intensive but essential for engineering and manufacturing. Recent advances in Large Language Models (LLMs) have inspired the LLM-based CAD generation by representing CAD as command sequences. But these methods struggle in practical scenarios because command sequence representation does not support entity selection (e.g. faces or edges), limiting its ability to support complex editing operations such as chamfer or fillet. Further, the discretization of a continuous variable during sketch and extrude operations may result in topological errors. To address these limitations, we present Pointer-CAD, a novel LLM-based CAD generation framework that leverages a pointer-based command sequence representation to explicitly incorporate the geometric information of B-rep models into sequential modeling. In particular, Pointer-CAD decomposes CAD model generation into steps, conditioning the generation of each subsequent step on both the textual description and the B-rep generated from previous steps. Whenever an operation requires the selection of a specific geometric entity, the LLM predicts a Pointer that selects the most feature-consistent candidate from the available set. Such a selection operation also reduces the quantization error in the command sequence-based representation. To support the training of Pointer-CAD, we develop a data annotation pipeline that produces expert-level natural language descriptions and apply it to build a dataset of approximately 575K CAD models. Extensive experimental results demonstrate that Pointer-CAD effectively supports the generation of complex geometric structures and reduces segmentation error to an extremely low level, achieving a significant improvement over prior command sequence methods, thereby significantly mitigating the topological inaccuracies introduced by quantization error.

</details>


### [82] [ArtHOI: Articulated Human-Object Interaction Synthesis by 4D Reconstruction from Video Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04338)
*Zihao Huang,Tianqi Liu,Zhaoxi Chen,Shaocong Xu,Saining Zhang,Lixing Xiao,Zhiguo Cao,Wei Li,Hao Zhao,Ziwei Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ArtHOI：首个通过视频先验进行4D重建的零样本关节式人-物交互合成框架，无需3D/4D监督，实现物理合理的关节交互


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零样本方法主要局限于刚性物体操作，缺乏显式的4D几何推理。关节式人-物交互合成在无3D/4D监督下仍具挑战，需要将生成与几何感知重建相结合

Method: 将关节式HOI合成视为从单目视频先验的4D重建问题：1）基于光流的部件分割，利用光流作为几何线索分离动态与静态区域；2）解耦重建流程，先恢复物体关节状态，再基于重建物体状态合成人体运动

Result: 在多种关节场景（打开冰箱、橱柜、微波炉等）中，ArtHOI在接触精度、穿透减少和关节保真度方面显著优于先前方法，将零样本交互合成扩展到刚性操作之外

Conclusion: ArtHOI通过重建导向的合成方法，将基于视频的生成与几何感知重建相结合，产生语义对齐且物理基础的交互，实现了无需3D/4D监督的物理合理关节式人-物交互合成

Abstract: Synthesizing physically plausible articulated human-object interactions (HOI) without 3D/4D supervision remains a fundamental challenge. While recent zero-shot approaches leverage video diffusion models to synthesize human-object interactions, they are largely confined to rigid-object manipulation and lack explicit 4D geometric reasoning. To bridge this gap, we formulate articulated HOI synthesis as a 4D reconstruction problem from monocular video priors: given only a video generated by a diffusion model, we reconstruct a full 4D articulated scene without any 3D supervision. This reconstruction-based approach treats the generated 2D video as supervision for an inverse rendering problem, recovering geometrically consistent and physically plausible 4D scenes that naturally respect contact, articulation, and temporal coherence. We introduce ArtHOI, the first zero-shot framework for articulated human-object interaction synthesis via 4D reconstruction from video priors. Our key designs are: 1) Flow-based part segmentation: leveraging optical flow as a geometric cue to disentangle dynamic from static regions in monocular video; 2) Decoupled reconstruction pipeline: joint optimization of human motion and object articulation is unstable under monocular ambiguity, so we first recover object articulation, then synthesize human motion conditioned on the reconstructed object states. ArtHOI bridges video-based generation and geometry-aware reconstruction, producing interactions that are both semantically aligned and physically grounded. Across diverse articulated scenes (e.g., opening fridges, cabinets, microwaves), ArtHOI significantly outperforms prior methods in contact accuracy, penetration reduction, and articulation fidelity, extending zero-shot interaction synthesis beyond rigid manipulation through reconstruction-informed synthesis.

</details>


### [83] [Balancing Fidelity, Utility, and Privacy in Synthetic Cardiac MRI Generation: A Comparative Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04340)
*Madhura Edirisooriya,Dasuni Kawya,Ishan Kumarasinghe,Isuri Devindi,Mary M. Maleckar,Roshan Ragel,Isuru Nawinne,Vajira Thambawita*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 系统评估三种生成模型（DDPM、LDM、FM）在心脏MRI合成数据生成中的表现，重点关注保真度、实用性和隐私性三个维度，发现DDPM在有限数据条件下提供最佳平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心脏MRI深度学习面临数据稀缺和隐私法规的双重约束，需要开发既能保护隐私又能有效增强数据的合成方法。

Method: 采用两阶段流程：首先使用解剖掩码作为条件，然后评估三种生成架构（DDPM、LDM、FM）在合成CMR生成中的表现，从保真度、实用性和隐私性三个维度进行系统基准测试。

Result: 基于扩散的模型（特别是DDPM）在有限数据条件下提供了下游分割实用性、图像保真度和隐私保护之间的最佳平衡；FM显示出有前景的隐私特性，但任务级性能略低。

Conclusion: 量化了跨域泛化和患者机密性之间的权衡，为医学影像中安全有效的合成数据增强建立了框架，DDPM是当前最优选择。

Abstract: Deep learning in cardiac MRI (CMR) is fundamentally constrained by both data scarcity and privacy regulations. This study systematically benchmarks three generative architectures: Denoising Diffusion Probabilistic Models (DDPM), Latent Diffusion Models (LDM), and Flow Matching (FM) for synthetic CMR generation. Utilizing a two-stage pipeline where anatomical masks condition image synthesis, we evaluate generated data across three critical axes: fidelity, utility, and privacy. Our results show that diffusion-based models, particularly DDPM, provide the most effective balance between downstream segmentation utility, image fidelity, and privacy preservation under limited-data conditions, while FM demonstrates promising privacy characteristics with slightly lower task-level performance. These findings quantify the trade-offs between cross-domain generalization and patient confidentiality, establishing a framework for safe and effective synthetic data augmentation in medical imaging.

</details>


### [84] [Hold-One-Shot-Out (HOSO) for Validation-Free Few-Shot CLIP Adapters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04341)
*Chris Vorster,Mayug Maniparambil,Noel E. O'Connor,Noel Murphy,Derek Molloy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HOSO-Adapter：一种无需验证集的CLIP适应方法，通过单样本留出机制学习混合比例超参数，在验证自由少样本设置中显著优于基线方法


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CLIP适应方法需要通过在测试集上进行消融或使用额外验证集来选择混合比例超参数，这不符合严格的少样本学习要求。需要一种无需验证集的方法来学习预训练CLIP知识与少样本监督之间的平衡参数。

Method: 提出Hold-One-Shot-Out (HOSO)方法：使用一个样本作为留出集来学习混合比例，适配器在剩余的少样本支持示例上进行训练。采用解耦训练策略，实现验证自由的CLIP适应。

Result: 在验证自由少样本协议下，HOSO-Adapter在11个标准少样本数据集上平均比CLIP-Adapter基线提升超过4个百分点。在8-shot和16-shot设置中，甚至优于在测试集上选择最优混合比例的CLIP-Adapter。

Conclusion: HOSO-Adapter提供了一种简单有效的验证自由CLIP适应方法，通过单样本留出机制成功学习混合比例，在少样本学习中实现了更好的性能，无需额外验证集或测试集调优。

Abstract: In many CLIP adaptation methods, a blending ratio hyperparameter controls the trade-off between general pretrained CLIP knowledge and the limited, dataset-specific supervision from the few-shot cases. Most few-shot CLIP adaptation techniques report results by ablation of the blending ratio on the test set or require additional validation sets to select the blending ratio per dataset, and thus are not strictly few-shot. We present a simple, validation-free method for learning the blending ratio in CLIP adaptation. Hold-One-Shot-Out (HOSO) presents a novel approach for CLIP-Adapter-style methods to compete in the newly established validation-free setting. CLIP-Adapter with HOSO (HOSO-Adapter) learns the blending ratio using a one-shot, hold-out set, while the adapter trains on the remaining few-shot support examples. Under the validation-free few-shot protocol, HOSO-Adapter outperforms the CLIP-Adapter baseline by more than 4 percentage points on average across 11 standard few-shot datasets. Interestingly, in the 8- and 16-shot settings, HOSO-Adapter outperforms CLIP-Adapter even with the optimal blending ratio selected on the test set. Ablation studies validate the use of a one-shot hold-out mechanism, decoupled training, and improvements over the naively learnt blending ratio baseline. Code is released here: https://github.com/chris-vorster/HOSO-Adapter

</details>


### [85] [Enhancing Authorship Attribution with Synthetic Paintings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04343)
*Clarissa Loures,Caio Hosken,Luan Oliveira,Gianlucca Zuin,Adriano Veloso*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 使用DreamBooth微调Stable Diffusion生成的合成图像，结合真实画作数据，可提升绘画作者归属分类模型的性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 绘画作者归属鉴定面临真实艺术品训练数据稀缺的挑战，需要探索合成图像能否提升计算模型的性能

Method: 提出混合方法：通过DreamBooth微调Stable Diffusion生成合成图像，结合真实画作数据训练分类模型

Result: 实验结果显示，添加合成图像相比仅使用真实画作，能获得更高的ROC-AUC和准确率

Conclusion: 通过整合生成式和判别式方法，该研究为数据稀缺场景下的艺术品认证计算机视觉技术发展做出贡献

Abstract: Attributing authorship to paintings is a historically complex task, and one of its main challenges is the limited availability of real artworks for training computational models. This study investigates whether synthetic images, generated through DreamBooth fine-tuning of Stable Diffusion, can improve the performance of classification models in this context. We propose a hybrid approach that combines real and synthetic data to enhance model accuracy and generalization across similar artistic styles. Experimental results show that adding synthetic images leads to higher ROC-AUC and accuracy compared to using only real paintings. By integrating generative and discriminative methods, this work contributes to the development of computer vision techniques for artwork authentication in data-scarce scenarios.

</details>


### [86] [Underrepresented in Foundation Model Pretraining Data? A One-Shot Probe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04346)
*Chris Vorster,Mayug Maniparambil,Noel E. O'Connor,Noel Murphy,Derek Molloy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种数据高效的方法，仅需每类一张标注图像即可预测视觉语言基础模型在目标领域的零样本准确率，使用大语言模型生成反事实描述并基于相似度特征训练线性回归器


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言基础模型在专业领域、新兴领域或代表性不足领域（特别是全球南方地区）的性能表现不一致，而评估这些模型通常需要标注测试集，这在特定领域往往不可得

Method: 使用大语言模型为每张图像生成合理的反事实描述，通过测量VLFM区分正确描述与这些困难负例的能力，构建捕捉其在共享嵌入空间中判别能力的特征，训练线性回归器预测零样本测试准确率

Result: 该方法在五个多样化数据集（包括标准基准数据集和非洲代表性不足数据集）上达到皮尔逊相关系数0.96，能够可靠预测VLFM的零样本准确率

Conclusion: 提供了一种低成本、可靠的工具来探测VLFM性能，使研究者和实践者能够在投入大量资源进行数据标注前做出明智决策

Abstract: Large-scale Vision-Language Foundation Models (VLFMs), such as CLIP, now underpin a wide range of computer vision research and applications. VLFMs are often adapted to various domain-specific tasks. However, VLFM performance on novel, specialised, or underrepresented domains remains inconsistent. Evaluating VLFMs typically requires labelled test sets, which are often unavailable for niche domains of interest, particularly those from the Global South. We address this gap by proposing a highly data-efficient method to predict a VLFM's zero-shot accuracy on a target domain using only a single labelled image per class. Our approach uses a Large Language Model to generate plausible counterfactual descriptions of a given image. By measuring the VLFM's ability to distinguish the correct description from these hard negatives, we engineer features that capture the VLFM's discriminative power in its shared embedding space. A linear regressor trained on these similarity scores estimates the VLFM's zero-shot test accuracy across various visual domains with a Pearson-r correlation of 0.96. We demonstrate our method's performance across five diverse datasets, including standard benchmark datasets and underrepresented datasets from Africa. Our work provides a low-cost, reliable tool for probing VLFMs, enabling researchers and practitioners to make informed decisions about data annotation efforts before committing significant resources. The model training code, generated captions and counterfactuals are released here: https://github.com/chris-vorster/PreLabellingProbe.

</details>


### [87] [RANGER: Sparsely-Gated Mixture-of-Experts with Adaptive Retrieval Re-ranking for Pathology Report Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04348)
*Yixin Chen,Ziyu Su,Hikmat Khan,Muhammad Khalid Khan Niazi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RANGER：一种用于病理报告生成的稀疏门控混合专家框架，通过动态专家路由和自适应检索重排序提升生成质量


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有病理报告生成框架通常采用同质解码器架构和静态知识检索集成，限制了生成专业化并可能在生成过程中引入噪声外部指导。需要解决WSI的千兆像素尺度和复杂形态异质性带来的挑战。

Method: 提出RANGER框架：1) 在解码器中集成稀疏门控混合专家(MoE)，采用噪声top-k路由和负载均衡正则化，实现跨不同诊断模式的动态专家专业化；2) 引入自适应检索重排序模块，在集成前选择性精炼从知识库检索的记忆，基于视觉特征表示减少噪声并改善语义对齐。

Result: 在PathText-BRCA数据集上进行了广泛实验，相比现有方法在标准自然语言生成指标上取得一致改进。完整RANGER模型在PathText数据集上达到最优性能：BLEU-1至BLEU-4分别为0.4598、0.3044、0.2036、0.1435，METEOR为0.1883，ROUGE-L为0.3038。

Conclusion: 动态专家路由和自适应知识精炼对于语义基础的病理报告生成是有效的，RANGER框架通过稀疏门控MoE和自适应检索重排序解决了现有方法的局限性。

Abstract: Pathology report generation remains a relatively under-explored downstream task, primarily due to the gigapixel scale and complex morphological heterogeneity of Whole Slide Images (WSIs). Existing pathology report generation frameworks typically employ transformer architectures, relying on a homogeneous decoder architecture and static knowledge retrieval integration. Such architectures limit generative specialization and may introduce noisy external guidance during the report generation process. To address these limitations, we propose RANGER, a sparsely-gated Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) framework with adaptive retrieval re-ranking for pathology report generation. Specifically, we integrate a sparsely gated MoE into the decoder, along with noisy top-$k$ routing and load-balancing regularization, to enable dynamic expert specialization across various diagnostic patterns. Additionally, we introduce an adaptive retrieval re-ranking module that selectively refines retrieved memory from a knowledge base before integration, reducing noise and improving semantic alignment based on visual feature representations. We perform extensive experiments on the PathText-BRCA dataset and demonstrate consistent improvements over existing approaches across standard natural language generation metrics. Our full RANGER model achieves optimal performance on PathText dataset, reaching BLEU-1 to BLEU-4 scores of 0.4598, 0.3044, 0.2036, and 0.1435, respectively, with METEOR of 0.1883, and ROUGE-L of 0.3038, validating the effectiveness of dynamic expert routing and adaptive knowledge refinement for semantically grounded pathology report generation.

</details>


### [88] [FocusGraph: Graph-Structured Frame Selection for Embodied Long Video Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04349)
*Tatiana Zemskova,Solomon Andryushenko,Ilya Obrubov,Viktoriia Khoruzhaia,Ekaterina Eroshenko,Ekaterina Derevyanka,Dmitry Yudin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FocusGraph：用于长视频问答的关键帧选择框架，通过场景描述LLM选择器和无训练补丁稀疏流保持方法，在保持性能的同时显著减少推理时间


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视频理解对具身智能体至关重要，但现有多模态大语言模型在处理长视频时面临响应质量下降和推理时间增长的问题，需要高效的关键帧选择方法

Method: 提出FocusGraph框架：1) 轻量级可训练的场景描述LLM选择器，基于图式描述选择查询相关片段；2) 无训练的补丁稀疏流保持方法，从选定片段中选择关键帧；3) 将关键帧输入MLLM生成最终答案

Result: 在FindingDory和HourVideo等具有挑战性的自我中心长视频问答基准测试中达到最先进水平，同时相对于基线方法显著减少推理时间

Conclusion: FocusGraph通过基于文本场景表示的关键帧选择，有效解决了长视频理解中MLLM性能下降和计算成本高的问题，为具身智能体的长时感知记忆处理提供了高效解决方案

Abstract: The ability to understand long videos is vital for embodied intelligent agents, because their effectiveness depends on how well they can accumulate, organize, and leverage long-horizon perceptual memories. Recently, multimodal LLMs have been gaining popularity for solving the long video understanding task due to their general ability to understand natural language and to leverage world knowledge. However, as the number of frames provided to an MLLM increases, the quality of its responses tends to degrade, and inference time grows. Therefore, when using MLLMs for long video understanding, a crucial step is selecting key frames from the video to answer user queries.
  In this work, we develop FocusGraph, a framework for keyframe selection for question answering over long egocentric videos. It leverages a lightweight trainable Scene-Caption LLM Selector that selects query-relevant clips based on their graph-based captions, and a training-free method for selecting keyframes from these clips. Unlike existing methods, the proposed Scene-Caption LLM Selector does not rely on the original sequence of low-resolution frames; instead, it operates on a compact textual representation of the scene. We then design a training-free Patch-wise Sparse-Flow Retention (PSFR) method to select keyframes from the resulting sequence of clips, which are fed into an MLLM to produce the final answer. Together, these components enable FocusGraph to achieve state-of-the-art results on challenging egocentric long-video question answering benchmarks, including FindingDory and HourVideo, while significantly reducing inference time relative to baseline approaches.

</details>


### [89] [Helios: Real Real-Time Long Video Generation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04379)
*Shenghai Yuan,Yuanyang Yin,Zongjian Li,Xinwei Huang,Xiao Yang,Li Yuan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Helios是首个14B参数视频生成模型，在单H100 GPU上实现19.5 FPS实时生成，支持分钟级视频生成，质量媲美强基线，无需传统防漂移启发式方法或标准加速技术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决长视频生成中的漂移问题、实现实时生成效率、简化训练基础设施，同时保持高质量视频生成能力。

Method: 采用14B自回归扩散模型，统一输入表示支持T2V/I2V/V2V任务；通过模拟漂移的专门训练策略解决长视频漂移；压缩历史噪声上下文减少采样步骤；基础设施级优化加速推理和训练。

Result: 在单H100 GPU上达到19.5 FPS实时生成，计算成本低于1.3B模型，长视频生成质量优于先前方法，无需传统防漂移启发式方法。

Conclusion: Helios在长视频生成质量、实时生成效率和训练基础设施简化方面取得突破，计划开源代码和模型以支持社区发展。

Abstract: We introduce Helios, the first 14B video generation model that runs at 19.5 FPS on a single NVIDIA H100 GPU and supports minute-scale generation while matching the quality of a strong baseline. We make breakthroughs along three key dimensions: (1) robustness to long-video drifting without commonly used anti-drifting heuristics such as self-forcing, error-banks, or keyframe sampling; (2) real-time generation without standard acceleration techniques such as KV-cache, sparse/linear attention, or quantization; and (3) training without parallelism or sharding frameworks, enabling image-diffusion-scale batch sizes while fitting up to four 14B models within 80 GB of GPU memory. Specifically, Helios is a 14B autoregressive diffusion model with a unified input representation that natively supports T2V, I2V, and V2V tasks. To mitigate drifting in long-video generation, we characterize typical failure modes and propose simple yet effective training strategies that explicitly simulate drifting during training, while eliminating repetitive motion at its source. For efficiency, we heavily compress the historical and noisy context and reduce the number of sampling steps, yielding computational costs comparable to -- or lower than -- those of 1.3B video generative models. Moreover, we introduce infrastructure-level optimizations that accelerate both inference and training while reducing memory consumption. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Helios consistently outperforms prior methods on both short- and long-video generation. We plan to release the code, base model, and distilled model to support further development by the community.

</details>


### [90] [TaxonRL: Reinforcement Learning with Intermediate Rewards for Interpretable Fine-Grained Visual Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04380)
*Maximilian von Klinski,Maximilian Schall*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TaxonRL使用强化学习进行分层分类推理，通过Group Relative Policy Optimization和中间奖励机制，在细粒度视觉识别任务中超越人类表现


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统视觉语言模型在对比性细粒度分类推理方面存在困难，特别是在区分同一属或科内视觉相似物种时。需要一种能够进行结构化、可解释推理的方法来提升细粒度视觉识别能力。

Method: 提出TaxonRL方法，使用Group Relative Policy Optimization强化学习框架，通过中间奖励机制将推理过程分解为分层分类预测。模型被激励先推理物种级、属级和科级特征，再进行最终分类，形成透明可验证的决策过程。

Result: 在Birds-to-Words数据集上达到91.7%的平均准确率，超越人类表现（77.3%），并生成可解释的推理轨迹。在灵长类和海洋物种验证任务中表现出强大的跨域泛化能力。

Conclusion: 强制执行结构化分层推理为细粒度视觉识别提供了强大且可迁移的框架，不仅提升准确性，还产生透明可验证的决策过程。

Abstract: Traditional vision-language models struggle with contrastive fine-grained taxonomic reasoning, particularly when distinguishing between visually similar species within the same genus or family. We introduce TaxonRL, a reinforcement learning approach using Group Relative Policy Optimization with intermediate rewards that decomposes the reasoning process into hierarchical taxonomic predictions. Our method incentivizes models to explicitly reason about species-level, genus-level, and family-level features before making final classifications. This structured approach is designed not only to boost accuracy but also to yield a transparent, verifiable decision-making process. On the challenging Birds-to-Words dataset, TaxonRL achieves 91.7\% average accuracy, exceeding human performance (77.3\%) while generating interpretable reasoning traces. We demonstrate strong cross-domain generalization, showing substantial gains in primate and marine species verification. Our results establish that enforcing structured, hierarchical reasoning provides a powerful and transferable framework for fine-grained visual discrimination.

</details>


### [91] [ZipMap: Linear-Time Stateful 3D Reconstruction with Test-Time Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04385)
*Haian Jin,Rundi Wu,Tianyuan Zhang,Ruiqi Gao,Jonathan T. Barron,Noah Snavely,Aleksander Holynski*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ZipMap是一种状态前馈模型，通过线性时间双向3D重建实现高效处理，在单次前向传播中将整个图像集合压缩为紧凑隐藏场景状态，比现有方法快20倍以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前馈变换器模型（如VGGT和π³）的计算成本随输入图像数量呈二次方增长，在处理大规模图像集合时效率低下。顺序重建方法虽然降低了计算成本，但牺牲了重建质量。

Method: ZipMap采用状态前馈模型架构，使用测试时训练层在单次前向传播中将整个图像集合"压缩"为紧凑的隐藏场景状态，实现线性时间的双向3D重建。

Result: ZipMap在单块H100 GPU上能在10秒内重建超过700帧，比VGGT等最先进方法快20倍以上，同时匹配或超越了二次方时间方法的精度。还展示了状态表示在实时场景状态查询和顺序流式重建中的优势。

Conclusion: ZipMap通过线性时间双向3D重建，在保持或超越现有方法精度的同时，显著提高了计算效率，为大规模图像集合的3D重建提供了高效解决方案，并展示了状态表示在实时应用中的潜力。

Abstract: Feed-forward transformer models have driven rapid progress in 3D vision, but state-of-the-art methods such as VGGT and $π^3$ have a computational cost that scales quadratically with the number of input images, making them inefficient when applied to large image collections. Sequential-reconstruction approaches reduce this cost but sacrifice reconstruction quality. We introduce ZipMap, a stateful feed-forward model that achieves linear-time, bidirectional 3D reconstruction while matching or surpassing the accuracy of quadratic-time methods. ZipMap employs test-time training layers to zip an entire image collection into a compact hidden scene state in a single forward pass, enabling reconstruction of over 700 frames in under 10 seconds on a single H100 GPU, more than $20\times$ faster than state-of-the-art methods such as VGGT. Moreover, we demonstrate the benefits of having a stateful representation in real-time scene-state querying and its extension to sequential streaming reconstruction.

</details>


### [92] [SimpliHuMoN: Simplifying Human Motion Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04399)
*Aadya Agrawal,Alexander Schwing*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于Transformer的简单有效模型，用于统一处理人体运动预测中的轨迹预测和姿态预测任务，无需任务特定修改即可在多个基准上取得SOTA结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前人体运动预测包含轨迹预测和姿态预测两个任务，但现有方法多为针对单一任务的专用模型。将这些模型组合用于整体运动预测具有挑战性，且近期方法在各自任务的基准上表现不佳。需要一种能够统一处理这两个任务的简单有效模型。

Method: 提出基于Transformer的简单模型，使用堆叠的自注意力模块有效捕捉姿态内的空间依赖性和运动序列中的时间关系。该模型是端到端的，无需任务特定修改即可处理仅姿态、仅轨迹和组合预测任务。

Result: 在Human3.6M、AMASS、ETH-UCY和3DPW等多个基准数据集上进行广泛实验，证明该方法在所有任务上均达到最先进的结果。

Conclusion: 提出的Transformer模型简单而有效，能够统一处理人体运动预测中的不同任务，在多个基准上取得SOTA性能，证明了其多功能性和优越性。

Abstract: Human motion prediction combines the tasks of trajectory forecasting and human pose prediction. For each of the two tasks, specialized models have been developed. Combining these models for holistic human motion prediction is non-trivial, and recent methods have struggled to compete on established benchmarks for individual tasks. To address this, we propose a simple yet effective transformer-based model for human motion prediction. The model employs a stack of self-attention modules to effectively capture both spatial dependencies within a pose and temporal relationships across a motion sequence. This simple, streamlined, end-to-end model is sufficiently versatile to handle pose-only, trajectory-only, and combined prediction tasks without task-specific modifications. We demonstrate that this approach achieves state-of-the-art results across all tasks through extensive experiments on a wide range of benchmark datasets, including Human3.6M, AMASS, ETH-UCY, and 3DPW.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [93] [On Google's SynthID-Text LLM Watermarking System: Theoretical Analysis and Empirical Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03410)
*Romina Omidi,Yun Dong,Binghui Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 对SynthID-Text进行首次理论分析，揭示其检测性能与鲁棒性，发现均值评分易受锦标赛层数增加影响，贝叶斯评分鲁棒性更好，并证明伯努利分布参数为0.5时检测最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Google的SynthID-Text是首个生产就绪的LLM生成水印系统，采用新颖的锦标赛方法实现最先进的AI文本检测能力。本文旨在对其进行首次理论分析，深入理解其检测性能和鲁棒性，为分析有效水印移除策略和设计鲁棒水印技术开辟新途径。

Method: 1) 提出新的锦标赛采样算法用于水印嵌入；2) 基于引入的评分函数（贝叶斯或均值评分）设计检测策略；3) 统一支持失真和非失真水印方法。通过理论分析和实证验证相结合，证明均值评分对锦标赛层数增加具有固有脆弱性，并设计层膨胀攻击来破解系统。

Result: 理论证明均值评分易受锦标赛层数增加影响，贝叶斯评分在水印鲁棒性方面表现更优。证明伯努利分布参数设为0.5时达到最优水印检测性能。通过层膨胀攻击成功破解SynthID-Text，揭示了系统的安全漏洞。

Conclusion: 本文对SynthID-Text进行了首次全面理论分析，不仅深化了对该系统的理解，还为分析有效水印移除策略和设计鲁棒水印技术开辟了新方向。研究结果表明需要更强大的水印方案来抵御层膨胀等攻击，贝叶斯评分相比均值评分具有更好的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Google's SynthID-Text, the first ever production-ready generative watermark system for large language model, designs a novel Tournament-based method that achieves the state-of-the-art detectability for identifying AI-generated texts. The system's innovation lies in: 1) a new Tournament sampling algorithm for watermarking embedding, 2) a detection strategy based on the introduced score function (e.g., Bayesian or mean score), and 3) a unified design that supports both distortionary and non-distortionary watermarking methods. This paper presents the first theoretical analysis of SynthID-Text, with a focus on its detection performance and watermark robustness, complemented by empirical validation. For example, we prove that the mean score is inherently vulnerable to increased tournament layers, and design a layer inflation attack to break SynthID-Text. We also prove the Bayesian score offers improved watermark robustness w.r.t. layers and further establish that the optimal Bernoulli distribution for watermark detection is achieved when the parameter is set to 0.5. Together, these theoretical and empirical insights not only deepen our understanding of SynthID-Text, but also open new avenues for analyzing effective watermark removal strategies and designing robust watermarking techniques. Source code is available at https: //github.com/romidi80/Synth-ID-Empirical-Analysis.

</details>


### [94] [PRIVATEEDIT: A Privacy-Preserving Pipeline for Face-Centric Generative Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03412)
*Dipesh Tamboli,Vineet Punyamoorty,Atharv Pawar,Vaneet Aggarwal*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: PRIVATEEDIT是一个隐私保护的生成式AI编辑管道，通过设备端分割和掩码技术分离身份敏感区域与可编辑图像内容，实现高质量人脸编辑的同时保护生物特征数据不外泄。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前生成式图像编辑系统通常需要将高保真人脸图像上传至第三方模型，引发了生物特征隐私、数据滥用和用户同意等担忧。需要一种既能支持高质量编辑又能让用户控制其生物特征数据的解决方案。

Method: 采用设备端分割和掩码技术分离身份敏感区域与可编辑图像上下文，设计可调掩码机制让用户控制面部信息隐藏程度。系统无需访问或重新训练第三方模型，兼容多种商业API，通过隐私默认设计确保生物特征数据从不暴露或传输。

Result: 开发了一个隐私保护的编辑管道，支持专业和创意工作流，提供选择性匿名化的用户界面。系统在保护数字身份的同时保持编辑质量，源代码已开源。

Conclusion: 通过将隐私作为核心设计约束，PRIVATEEDIT为以用户自主权和信任为中心的负责任生成式AI提供了技术可行性和规范性指导，展示了隐私设计在生成式AI中的重要性。

Abstract: Recent advances in generative image editing have enabled transformative applications, from professional head shot generation to avatar stylization. However, these systems often require uploading high-fidelity facial images to third-party models, raising concerns around biometric privacy, data misuse, and user consent. We propose a privacy-preserving pipeline that supports high-quality editing while keeping users in control over their biometric data in face-centric use cases. Our approach separates identity-sensitive regions from editable image context using on-device segmentation and masking, enabling secure, user-controlled editing without modifying third-party generative models. Unlike traditional cloud-based tools, PRIVATEEDIT enforces privacy by default: biometric data is never exposed or transmitted. This design requires no access to or retraining of third-party models, making it compatible with a wide range of commercial APIs. By treating privacy as a core design constraint, our system supports responsible generative AI centered on user autonomy and trust. The pipeline includes a tunable masking mechanism that lets users control how much facial information is concealed, allowing them to balance privacy and output fidelity based on trust level or use case. We demonstrate its applicability in professional and creative workflows and provide a user interface for selective anonymization. By advocating privacy-by-design in generative AI, our work offers both technical feasibility and normative guidance for protecting digital identity. The source code is available at https://github.com/Dipeshtamboli/PrivateEdit-Privacy-Preserving-GenAI.

</details>


### [95] [Zero-Knowledge Federated Learning with Lattice-Based Hybrid Encryption for Quantum-Resilient Medical AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03398)
*Edouard Lansiaux*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: ZKFL-PQ：一种三层密码协议，结合ML-KEM、格基零知识证明和BFV同态加密，为联邦学习提供量子安全、可验证梯度完整性和隐私保护聚合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习在医疗AI协作训练中存在安全漏洞：梯度反转攻击可重建患者信息，拜占庭客户端可毒化全局模型，以及"现在收集、未来解密"威胁使当前加密流量易受未来量子攻击。

Method: 提出ZKFL-PQ三层密码协议：1) ML-KEM（FIPS 203）用于量子抵抗密钥封装；2) 格基零知识证明用于可验证的范数约束梯度完整性；3) BFV同态加密用于隐私保护聚合。在经典随机预言机模型下基于Module-LWE、Ring-LWE和SIS假设证明正确性和零知识特性。

Result: 在5个联邦客户端、10轮训练的合成医学影像数据上评估，协议实现100%拒绝范数违规更新，同时保持模型准确率100%，而标准FL准确率降至23%。计算开销约20倍，与临床研究每日/每周训练周期兼容。

Conclusion: ZKFL-PQ为医疗联邦学习提供量子安全、可验证完整性和隐私保护，当前防御保证拒绝大范数恶意更新，但对细微低范数或定向毒化的鲁棒性仍需未来研究。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) enables collaborative training of medical AI models across hospitals without centralizing patient data. However, the exchange of model updates exposes critical vulnerabilities: gradient inversion attacks can reconstruct patient information, Byzantine clients can poison the global model, and the \emph{Harvest Now, Decrypt Later} (HNDL) threat renders today's encrypted traffic vulnerable to future quantum adversaries.We introduce \textbf{ZKFL-PQ} (\emph{Zero-Knowledge Federated Learning, Post-Quantum}), a three-tiered cryptographic protocol that hybridizes (i) ML-KEM (FIPS~203) for quantum-resistant key encapsulation, (ii) lattice-based Zero-Knowledge Proofs for verifiable \emph{norm-constrained} gradient integrity, and (iii) BFV homomorphic encryption for privacy-preserving aggregation. We formalize the security model and prove correctness and zero-knowledge properties under the Module-LWE, Ring-LWE, and SIS assumptions \emph{in the classical random oracle model}. We evaluate ZKFL-PQ on synthetic medical imaging data across 5 federated clients over 10 training rounds. Our protocol achieves \textbf{100\% rejection of norm-violating updates} while maintaining model accuracy at 100\%, compared to a catastrophic drop to 23\% under standard FL. The computational overhead (factor $\sim$20$\times$) is analyzed and shown to be compatible with clinical research workflows operating on daily or weekly training cycles. We emphasize that the current defense guarantees rejection of large-norm malicious updates; robustness against subtle low-norm or directional poisoning remains future work.

</details>


### [96] [Parallel Test-Time Scaling with Multi-Sequence Verifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03417)
*Yegon Kim,Seungyoo Lee,Chaeyun Jang,Hyungi Lee,Juho Lee*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: MSV是一种多序列验证器，通过联合处理所有候选解决方案并建模其交互，解决了并行测试时扩展中的验证器校准问题，同时实现了新颖的早期停止框架以降低延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 并行测试时扩展技术虽然能提升大语言模型性能，但面临两个关键瓶颈：1）从候选池中准确选择正确解决方案；2）生成多个完整解决方案带来的高推理延迟。这两个问题都与验证器校准密切相关。

Method: 提出了多序列验证器（MSV），这是首个设计用于联合处理所有候选解决方案并建模其交互的验证器。还引入了流式MSV变体，支持新颖的早期停止框架，充分利用并行解码优势。

Result: MSV改善了验证器校准，直接提升了最佳候选选择性能。在早期停止框架中，MSV能够以大约一半的延迟实现相同的目标准确率，相比单独评分每个解决方案的验证器。

Conclusion: 通过联合建模候选解决方案之间的交互，MSV有效解决了并行测试时扩展中的验证器校准问题，同时通过新颖的早期停止框架显著降低了推理延迟，为大规模语言模型的高效推理提供了新方法。

Abstract: Parallel test-time scaling, which generates multiple candidate solutions for a single problem, is a powerful technique for improving large language model performance. However, it is hindered by two key bottlenecks: accurately selecting the correct solution from the candidate pool, and the high inference latency from generating many full solutions. We argue that both challenges are fundamentally linked to verifier calibration. A well-calibrated verifier not only improves answer selection, but also enables early-stopping strategies to reduce latency. However, existing verifiers are limited as they score each candidate in isolation, overlooking rich contextual information across the set of candidates. To address this, we introduce the Multi-Sequence Verifier (MSV), the first verifier designed to jointly process all candidate solutions and model their interactions. MSV achieves improved calibration, which directly enhances best-of-N selection performance. We further introduce a streaming MSV variant that empowers a novel early-stopping framework. Our novel framework fully leverages parallel decoding, which contrasts with the existing multi-sequence early exit works that decode sequences one by one and thus incur significant latency. In this novel setting, MSV can achieve the same target accuracy with around half the latency that would be required with its counterpart that scores each solution in isolation.

</details>


### [97] [Sharing is caring: Attestable and Trusted Workflows out of Distrustful Components](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03403)
*Amir Al Sadi,Sina Abdollahi,Adrien Ghosn,Hamed Haddadi,Marios Kogias*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Mica是一个机密计算架构，通过解耦机密性与信任，为租户提供定义、限制和认证组件间通信路径的机制，防止敏感数据通过共享资源泄漏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前可信执行环境（TEEs）对组件间安全通信支持不足，导致独立开发和部署的TEEs管道必须相互信任以避免敏感信息泄漏，这一脆弱假设不适用于现代云工作负载。

Method: 在Arm CCA上实现Mica架构，利用现有原语，仅需对可信计算基进行适度修改。扩展包括用于控制和认证通信路径的策略语言，支持Realm之间以及与不可信世界通过共享保护/非保护内存和控制转移的通信。

Result: 评估显示Mica能够支持现实的云管道，仅小幅增加可信计算基，同时提供强大且可认证的机密性保证。

Conclusion: Mica通过解耦机密性与信任，为机密计算提供了更安全的组件间通信机制，解决了当前TEEs在云工作负载中的安全通信限制问题。

Abstract: Confidential computing protects data in use within Trusted Execution Environments (TEEs), but current TEEs provide little support for secure communication between components. As a result, pipelines of independently developed and deployed TEEs must trust one another to avoid the leakage of sensitive information they exchange -- a fragile assumption that is unrealistic for modern cloud workloads.
  We present Mica, a confidential computing architecture that decouples confidentiality from trust. Mica provides tenants with explicit mechanisms to define, restrict, and attest all communication paths between components, ensuring that sensitive data cannot leak through shared resources or interactions. We implement Mica on Arm CCA using existing primitives, requiring only modest changes to the trusted computing base. Our extension adds a policy language to control and attest communication paths among Realms and with the untrusted world via shared protected and unprotected memory and control transfers.
  Our evaluation shows that Mica supports realistic cloud pipelines with only a small increase to the trusted computing base while providing strong, attestable confidentiality guarantees.

</details>


### [98] [Analyzing the Impact of Adversarial Attacks on C-V2X-Enabled Road Safety: An Age of Information Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03462)
*Mahmudul Hassan Ashik,Moinul Hossain*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种针对NR-V2X的新型资源饥饿攻击，利用资源分配漏洞降低自动驾驶系统的服务级别要求，并通过建立基于离散时间马尔可夫链的AoI分析模型来评估攻击对道路安全的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管NR-V2X旨在满足自动驾驶系统的服务级别要求以确保道路安全，但其资源分配机制中仍存在未充分研究的漏洞。现有研究主要基于成功的数据包接收来评估道路安全，缺乏对恶意攻击如何影响关键性能指标的深入分析。

Method: 提出了一种针对NR-V2X资源分配机制的新型资源饥饿攻击；建立了基于离散时间马尔可夫链的分析模型，将信息年龄作为评估攻击影响的主要指标；通过广泛的仿真验证了模型的准确性。

Result: 攻击能够将自动驾驶系统中时间敏感应用的服务级别要求降低高达15%；即使在良性车辆中，当资源稀缺时也会出现自私行为，进一步危及安全；AoI模型有效量化了攻击对系统性能的影响。

Conclusion: NR-V2X的资源分配机制存在严重安全漏洞，资源饥饿攻击能够显著降低自动驾驶系统的安全性能。需要开发更健壮的资源分配机制来抵御此类攻击，并考虑资源稀缺环境下车辆的自私行为对整体安全的影响。

Abstract: The Cellular Vehicle-to-Everything (C-V2X), introduced and developed by the 3GPP, is a promising technology for the Autonomous Driving System (ADS). C-V2X aims to fulfill the Service-Level Requirements (SLRs) of ADS to ensure road safety following the development of the latest version, i.e., the NR-V2X. However, vulnerabilities threatening road safety in NR-V2X persist that have yet to be investigated. Existing research primarily evaluates road safety based on successful packet receptions. In this work, we propose a novel resource starvation attack that exploits vulnerabilities in the resource allocation of NR-V2X to diminish the required SLRs, making the road condition unsafe for autonomous driving. Furthermore, we establish the Age of Information (AoI) as the predominant metric for estimating the impact of adversarial attacks on NR-V2X by constructing a Discrete-time Markov chain (DTMC) based analytical model and validating it through extensive simulations. Finally, our analysis underscores how the proposed attack on NR-V2X can lead to unsafe driving conditions by reducing the SLR of time-sensitive applications in ADS up to 15% from the target. Additionally, we observe that even benign vehicles act selfishly when resources are scarce, leading to further safety compromises.

</details>


### [99] [Scrambler: Mixed Boolean Arithmetic Obfuscation Tool Using E-graph and Equality Expansion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03624)
*Seoksu Lee,Sangjun An,Eun-Sun Cho*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Scrambler是一种基于e-graph的MBA混淆工具，使用等式扩展技术高效生成复杂多样的表达式，并通过构造保证等价性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MBA（混合布尔算术）混淆工具在表达式复杂性和多样性方面存在不足，需要一种能够高效生成复杂且多样化等价表达式的工具。

Method: 采用基于e-graph（等式图）的方法，结合等式扩展技术，通过构造保证生成表达式与原始表达式的等价性。

Result: 实验结果表明，Scrambler在表达能力和复杂性方面优于现有工具。

Conclusion: Scrambler是一种有效的MBA混淆工具，能够生成更复杂、更多样的等价表达式，提高了混淆效果。

Abstract: We propose Scrambler, and e-graph-based MBA obfuscation tool using Equality Expansion to efficiently generate complex and diverse expressions with equivalence guaranteed by construction. Experiments show Scrambler improves existing tools in expressiveness and complexity.

</details>


### [100] [Zero-Knowledge Proof (ZKP) Authentication for Offline CBDC Payment System Using IoT Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03804)
*Santanu Mondal,T. Chithralekha*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于安全元件、零知识证明和间歇同步的隐私保护离线CBDC模型，专门针对资源受限的物联网设备，实现无需互联网连接的离线支付。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CBDC需要提供安全的离线支付功能以实现金融包容性，但物联网设备资源受限，难以实现双重支付预防、隐私保护、低计算操作和数字身份管理等关键功能。

Method: 采用混合架构，结合在线和离线支付，利用安全硬件和轻量级零知识证明算法，通过安全元件、零知识证明和间歇同步机制实现物联网设备上的隐私保护离线CBDC支付。

Result: 提出了一个完整的隐私保护离线CBDC模型，能够在资源受限的物联网设备上实现安全的离线支付，同时满足反洗钱和反恐融资监管要求。

Conclusion: 该模型通过创新的技术组合解决了物联网设备上离线CBDC支付的关键挑战，为实现金融包容性和数字支付的普及提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Central Bank Digital Currency (CBDCs) are becoming a new digital financial tool aimed at financial inclusion, increased monetary stability, and improved efficiency of payment systems, as they are issued by central banks. One of the most important aspects is that the CBDC must offer secure offline payment methods to users, allowing them to retain cash-like access without violating Anti-Money Laundering and Counter-terrorism Financing (AML/CFT) rules. The offline CBDC ecosystems will provide financial inclusion, empower underserved communities, and ensure equitable access to digital payments, even in connectivity-poor remote locations. With the rapid growth of Internet of Things (IoT) devices in our everyday lives, they are capable of performing secure digital transactions. Integrating offline CBDC payment with IoT devices enables seamless, automated payment without internet connectivity. However, IoT devices face special challenges due to their resource-constrained nature. This makes it difficult to include features such as double-spending prevention, privacy preservation, low-computation operation, and digital identity management. The work proposes a privacy-preserving offline CBDC model with integrated secure elements (SEs), zero-knowledge proofs (ZKPs), and intermittent synchronisation to conduct offline payments on IoT hardware. The proposed model is based on recent improvements in offline CBDC prototypes, regulations and cryptographic design choices such as hybrid architecture that involves using combination of online and offline payment in IoT devices using secure hardware with lightweight zero-knowledge proof cryptographic algorithm.

</details>


### [101] [Measuring Privacy vs. Fidelity in Synthetic Social Media Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03906)
*Henry Tari,Adriana Iamnitchi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该研究评估了由大语言模型生成的Instagram合成帖子的隐私风险，提出了一种将重新识别视为作者归属攻击的隐私量化方法，揭示了合成文本中隐私与保真度之间的权衡关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交媒体数据需要代表性的合成等价物来支持研究并实现数据共享的可重复性，但现有研究主要关注表格合成数据的隐私风险，对非结构化文本合成数据的隐私风险了解甚少。

Method: 使用三种最先进的大语言模型和两种提示策略生成Instagram合成帖子；提出将重新识别视为作者归属攻击的隐私量化方法；使用RoBERTa-large分类器在真实帖子上训练，然后在合成帖子上测试；通过文本特征、情感分析、主题重叠和嵌入相似性评估保真度。

Result: 在真实数据上作者归属准确率达到81%，但在合成帖子上仅为16.5-29.7%，显示风险降低但仍不可忽视；保真度评估证实了预期的权衡：更高的保真度伴随着更大的隐私泄露。

Conclusion: 该研究为评估合成文本隐私提供了框架，并证明了社交媒体数据集中隐私与保真度之间的紧张关系，合成文本虽然降低了隐私风险，但并非完全隐私保护。

Abstract: Synthetic data is increasingly used to support research without exposing sensitive user content. Social media data is one of the types of datasets that would hugely benefit from representative synthetic equivalents that can be used to bootstrap research and allow reproducibility through data sharing. However, recent studies show that (tabular) synthetic data is not inherently privacy-preserving. Much less is known, however, about the privacy risks of synthetically generated unstructured texts. This work evaluates the privacy of synthetic Instagram posts generated by three state-of-the-art large language models using two prompting strategies. We propose a methodology that quantifies privacy by framing re-identification as an authorship attribution attack. A RoBERTa-large classifier trained on real posts achieved 81\% accuracy in authorship attribution on real data, but only 16.5--29.7\% on synthetic posts, showing reduced, though non-negligible, risk. Fidelity was assessed via text traits, sentiment, topic overlap, and embedding similarity, confirming the expected trade-off: higher fidelity coincides with greater privacy leakage. This work provides a framework for evaluating privacy in synthetic text and demonstrates the privacy--fidelity tension in social media datasets.

</details>


### [102] [OMNIINTENT: A Trusted Intent-Centric Framework for User-Friendly Web3](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04168)
*Zhuoran Pan,Yue Li,Zhi Guan,Jianbin Hu,Zhong Chen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: OMNIINTENT是一个语言-运行时协同设计的意图中心化DeFi系统，通过领域特定语言、TEE编译器、执行优化器和内存池感知可行性检查器，解决了现有方案在表达能力、信任、隐私和可组合性方面的权衡问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Web3和DeFi生态系统日益复杂，对参与者的技术专业知识和金融素养要求越来越高。现有的意图中心化方法（如类型化意图设计和LLM驱动的求解器）在表达能力、信任、隐私和可组合性之间存在权衡，需要一种能同时满足这些需求的新方案。

Method: 1. ICL（意图中心化语言）：用于精确灵活地指定触发器、操作和运行时约束的领域特定语言；2. TEE编译器：在可信执行环境（TEE）中将意图编译为签名、状态绑定的交易；3. 执行优化器：构建交易依赖图以实现安全的并行批量提交；4. 内存池感知可行性检查器：预测执行结果。

Result: 全栈原型处理多样化的DeFi场景，实现了89.6%的意图覆盖率，通过并行执行获得高达7.3倍的吞吐量加速，可行性预测准确率高达99.2%且延迟较低。

Conclusion: OMNIINTENT通过语言-运行时协同设计成功调和了意图中心化DeFi系统中的表达能力、信任、隐私和可组合性要求，为复杂Web3生态系统提供了高效、安全且用户友好的解决方案。

Abstract: The increasingly complex Web3 ecosystem and decentralized finance (DeFi) landscape demand ever higher levels of technical expertise and financial literacy from participants. The Intent-Centric paradigm in DeFi has thus emerged in response, which allows users to focus on their trading intents rather than the underlying execution details. However, existing approaches, including Typed-intent design and LLM-driven solver, trade off expressiveness, trust, privacy, and composability.
  We present OMNIINTENT, a language-runtime co-design that reconciles these requirements. OMNIINTENT introduces ICL, a domain-specific Intent-Centric Language for precise yet flexible specification of triggers, actions, and runtime constraints; a Trusted Execution Environment (TEE)-based compiler that compiles intents into signed, state-bound transactions inside an enclave; and an execution optimizer that constructs transaction dependency graphs for safe parallel batch submission and a mempool-aware feasibility checker that predicts execution outcomes. Our full-stack prototype processes diverse DeFi scenarios, achieving 89.6% intent coverage, up to 7.3x throughput speedup via parallel execution, and feasibility-prediction accuracy up to 99.2% with low latency.

</details>


### [103] [Statistical Effort Modelling of Game Resource Localisation Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04261)
*Alessandro Sanna,Waldo Verstraete,Leonardo Regano,Davide Maiorca,Bjorn De Sutter*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文完整实例化了获取统计工作量模型的方法，用于游戏资源本地化攻击，验证了该方法在需要人机交互的MATE攻击中的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MATE软件保护（如代码混淆）的有效性证据主要来自有限的实证研究。虽然最近提出了可自动化的方法来获取攻击（受保护）软件所需工作量的统计模型，但该方法尚未针对需要人机交互的攻击策略进行实例化、评估或验证。

Method: 完整实例化了获取统计工作量模型的方法，专门针对游戏资源本地化攻击（这是创建游戏作弊的主要步骤）。详细讨论了实例化的所有相关方面，并在两个游戏用例中应用该方法。

Result: 结果证实了所提方法的可行性及其对软件保护工具用户的决策支持效用。该方法为获取软件保护对逆向工程攻击影响的模型开辟了新途径，比涉及人类参与者的实证研究更具扩展性。

Conclusion: 该方法成功实例化并验证了针对需要人机交互的MATE攻击的统计工作量建模方法，为软件保护效果评估提供了可扩展的新方法，能够更好地支持保护工具用户的决策过程。

Abstract: Evidence on the effectiveness of Man-At-The-End (MATE) software protections, such as code obfuscation, has mainly come from limited empirical research. Recently, however, an automatable method was proposed to obtain statistical models of the required effort to attack (protected) software. The proposed method was sketched for a number of attack strategies but not instantiated, evaluated, or validated for those that require human interaction with the attacked software.
  In this paper, we present a full instantiation of the method to obtain statistical effort models for game resource localisation attacks, which represent a major step towards creating game cheats, a prime example of MATE attacks. We discuss in detail all relevant aspects of our instantiation and the results obtained for two game use cases. Our results confirm the feasibility of the proposed method and its utility for decision support for users of software protection tools. These results open up a new avenue for obtaining models of the impact of software protections on reverse engineering attacks, which will scale much better than empirical research involving human participants.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [104] [The Theory behind UMAP?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03375)
*David Wegmann*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该论文旨在修正Spivak原始草稿和McInnes等人UMAP论文中的数学错误，提供完整的度量实现函子推导，并讨论UMAP算法及其性质


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Spivak关于度量实现函子的未发表草稿存在许多错误，这些错误被McInnes等人在UMAP论文中复制并传播。需要修正这些数学错误，提供完整的推导过程，并澄清UMAP算法与有限变体之间的对应关系

Method: 通过修复Spivak草稿中的错误，提供度量实现函子及相关函子的显式描述，建立自包含的数学推导文档，并分析McInnes等人的有限变体与UMAP算法的对应关系

Result: 提供了修正后的度量实现函子完整推导，给出了函子的显式描述，澄清了数学错误，并讨论了UMAP算法的性质及其与有限变体的对应关系

Conclusion: 成功修复了Spivak草稿和UMAP论文中的数学错误，提供了完整的度量实现函子理论框架，为UMAP算法的数学基础提供了更严谨的支撑

Abstract: In 2018, McInnes et al. introduced a dimensionality reduction algorithm called UMAP, which enjoys wide popularity among data scientists. Their work introduces a finite variant of a functor called the metric realization, based on an unpublished draft by Spivak. This draft contains many errors, most of which are reproduced by McInnes et al. and subsequent publications. This article aims to repair these errors and provide a self-contained document with the full derivation of Spivak's functors and McInnes et al.'s finite variant. We contribute an explicit description of the metric realization and related functors. At the end, we discuss the UMAP algorithm, as well as claims about properties of the algorithm and the correspondence of McInnes et al.'s finite variant to the UMAP algorithm.

</details>


### [105] [Learning Order Forest for Qualitative-Attribute Data Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03387)
*Mingjie Zhao,Sen Feng,Yiqun Zhang,Mengke Li,Yang Lu,Yiu-ming Cheung*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出一种基于树状结构的距离表示方法，用于处理定性属性值的聚类问题，通过联合学习机制同时优化树结构和聚类结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统聚类方法通常基于欧氏距离空间，但对于定性属性值（如症状、婚姻状况等名义值）的隐式聚类分布，欧氏距离并不适用。需要一种能够灵活表示定性值之间局部顺序关系的距离结构。

Method: 提出树状距离结构来表示定性值之间的局部顺序关系，将每个值视为树的顶点。采用联合学习机制迭代优化树结构和聚类结果，最终形成由学习得到的树组成的森林来表示整个数据集的潜在距离空间。

Result: 在12个真实基准数据集上与10个对比方法进行比较，并通过显著性检验验证了所提方法的优越性。实验表明联合学习能够使森林适应聚类任务，产生准确的结果。

Conclusion: 提出的树状距离结构和联合学习机制能够有效处理定性属性值的聚类问题，通过捕获丰富的顺序关系并在聚类友好的形式下学习树结构，显著提升了聚类性能。

Abstract: Clustering is a fundamental approach to understanding data patterns, wherein the intuitive Euclidean distance space is commonly adopted. However, this is not the case for implicit cluster distributions reflected by qualitative attribute values, e.g., the nominal values of attributes like symptoms, marital status, etc. This paper, therefore, discovered a tree-like distance structure to flexibly represent the local order relationship among intra-attribute qualitative values. That is, treating a value as the vertex of the tree allows to capture rich order relationships among the vertex value and the others. To obtain the trees in a clustering-friendly form, a joint learning mechanism is proposed to iteratively obtain more appropriate tree structures and clusters. It turns out that the latent distance space of the whole dataset can be well-represented by a forest consisting of the learned trees. Extensive experiments demonstrate that the joint learning adapts the forest to the clustering task to yield accurate results. Comparisons of 10 counterparts on 12 real benchmark datasets with significance tests verify the superiority of the proposed method.

</details>


### [106] [Beyond Cross-Validation: Adaptive Parameter Selection for Kernel-Based Gradient Descents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03401)
*Xiaotong Liu,Yunwen Lei,Xiangyu Chang,Shao-Bo Lin*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出一种基于偏差-方差分析和分裂方法的核梯度下降参数选择策略，引入经验有效维度概念，实现自适应参数选择，达到最优泛化误差界


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有核梯度下降算法的参数选择方法存在不足，需要一种能够自适应不同核函数、目标函数和误差度量的参数选择策略，以提升算法性能

Method: 结合偏差-方差分析和分裂方法，引入经验有效维度概念量化迭代增量，提出可实施的自适应参数选择策略，并在学习理论框架下进行理论验证

Result: 理论证明表明，采用所提自适应参数选择策略的核梯度下降算法能够达到最优泛化误差界，并能有效适应不同核函数、目标函数和误差度量

Conclusion: 所提出的自适应参数选择策略相比现有方法具有显著优势，为核梯度下降算法提供了有效的参数选择方案

Abstract: This paper proposes a novel parameter selection strategy for kernel-based gradient descent (KGD) algorithms, integrating bias-variance analysis with the splitting method. We introduce the concept of empirical effective dimension to quantify iteration increments in KGD, deriving an adaptive parameter selection strategy that is implementable. Theoretical verifications are provided within the framework of learning theory. Utilizing the recently developed integral operator approach, we rigorously demonstrate that KGD, equipped with the proposed adaptive parameter selection strategy, achieves the optimal generalization error bound and adapts effectively to different kernels, target functions, and error metrics. Consequently, this strategy showcases significant advantages over existing parameter selection methods for KGD.

</details>


### [107] [Surprisal-Rényi Free Energy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03405)
*Shion Matsumoto,Raul Castillo,Benjamin Prada,Ankur Arjun Mali*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 论文提出了Surprisal-Rényi自由能(SRFE)，这是一个基于对数矩的似然比泛函，能够统一描述前向和反向KL散度的极限行为，并揭示控制偏离KL主导机制的显式均值-方差权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前向和反向KL散度在学习和推理中作为极限目标出现，但产生明显不同的归纳偏差，这些偏差无法仅通过期望水平来解释。需要一个新的理论框架来理解这两种KL散度极限的几何和大偏差结构。

Method: 引入Surprisal-Rényi自由能(SRFE)，这是一个基于对数矩的似然比泛函，不属于f-散度类。证明SRFE能够恢复前向和反向KL散度作为奇异端点极限，并在两个极限附近推导局部展开，其中对数似然比的方差作为一阶修正项出现。

Result: 1. 揭示了控制偏离KL主导机制的显式均值-方差权衡；2. 建立了SRFE的Gibbs型变分特征化，证明其为KL散度加权和的唯一最小化器；3. 证明了SRFE通过Chernoff型边界直接控制超额编码长度的大偏差，提供了精确的最小描述长度解释。

Conclusion: SRFE作为一个对方差和尾部敏感的自由能泛函，阐明了前向和反向KL极限的几何和大偏差结构，但并未统一或取代不同的学习框架。该框架为理解KL散度极限行为提供了新的理论视角。

Abstract: The forward and reverse Kullback-Leibler (KL) divergences arise as limiting objectives in learning and inference yet induce markedly different inductive biases that cannot be explained at the level of expectations alone. In this work, we introduce the Surprisal-Rényi Free Energy (SRFE), a log-moment-based functional of the likelihood ratio that lies outside the class of $f$-divergences. We show that SRFE recovers forward and reverse KL divergences as singular endpoint limits and derive local expansions around both limits in which the variance of the log-likelihood ratio appears as a first-order correction. This reveals an explicit mean-variance tradeoff governing departures from KL-dominated regimes. We further establish a Gibbs-type variational characterization of SRFE as the unique minimizer of a weighted sum of KL divergences and prove that SRFE directly controls large deviations of excess code-length via Chernoff-type bounds, yielding a precise Minimum Description Length interpretation. Together, these results identify SRFE as a variance- and tail-sensitive free-energy functional that clarifies the geometric and large-deviation structure underlying forward and reverse KL limits, without unifying or subsuming distinct learning frameworks.

</details>


### [108] [Scalable Contrastive Causal Discovery under Unknown Soft Interventions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03411)
*Mingxuan Zhang,Khushi Desai,Sopho Kevlishvili,Elham Azizi*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出了一种可扩展的因果发现模型，用于处理具有共享因果结构和未知软干预的配对观测与干预设置，通过聚合子集级PDAG和应用对比性跨机制定向规则来构建全局一致的极大PDAG。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 观测因果发现仅能识别到马尔可夫等价类，而干预可以减少这种模糊性。但在实践中，干预通常是具有多个未知目标的软干预，且通常只能观察到单一干预机制。需要一种能够处理未知软干预、可扩展到更大图结构的方法。

Method: 提出一个可扩展模型，用于配对观测和干预设置：1) 聚合子集级部分有向无环图(PDAGs)；2) 应用对比性跨机制定向规则；3) 在Meek闭包下构建全局一致的极大PDAG。该方法允许泛化到分布内和分布外设置。

Result: 理论证明：模型在子集限制设置诱导的受限Ψ等价类中是完备的；渐近恢复可识别的PDAG；相比非对比性子集限制方法能定向更多边。实验显示：改进因果结构恢复；泛化到具有保留因果机制的未见图；可扩展到更大图。

Conclusion: 提出的模型能够有效处理具有未知软干预的配对观测-干预设置，通过对比性跨机制定向实现更好的因果发现，在理论和实验上都表现出优越性，特别是在可扩展性和泛化能力方面。

Abstract: Observational causal discovery is only identifiable up to the Markov equivalence class. While interventions can reduce this ambiguity, in practice interventions are often soft with multiple unknown targets. In many realistic scenarios, only a single intervention regime is observed. We propose a scalable causal discovery model for paired observational and interventional settings with shared underlying causal structure and unknown soft interventions. The model aggregates subset-level PDAGs and applies contrastive cross-regime orientation rules to construct a globally consistent maximal PDAG under Meek closure, enabling generalization to both in-distribution and out-of-distribution settings. Theoretically, we prove that our model is sound with respect to a restricted $Ψ$ equivalence class induced solely by the information available in the subset-restricted setting. We further show that the model asymptotically recovers the corresponding identifiable PDAG and can orient additional edges compared to non-contrastive subset-restricted methods. Experiments on synthetic data demonstrate improved causal structure recovery, generalization to unseen graphs with held-out causal mechanisms, and scalability to larger graphs, with ablations supporting the theoretical results.

</details>


### [109] [Riemannian Langevin Dynamics: Strong Convergence of Geometric Euler-Maruyama Scheme](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03626)
*Zhiyuan Zhan,Masashi Sugiyama*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该论文研究了黎曼流形上随机微分方程的几何欧拉-丸山格式，证明了在几何和正则性条件下具有1/2阶强收敛性，并应用于黎曼朗之万动力学的采样。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界数据中的低维结构对生成模型成功至关重要，这促使了在内在数据流形上定义扩散模型。这类模型由流形上的随机微分方程驱动，因此需要流形值SDE数值格式的收敛理论。在欧几里得空间中，欧拉-丸山格式达到1/2阶强收敛，但在一般设置下流形离散化的类似结果较少被理解。

Method: 研究黎曼流形上SDE的几何欧拉-丸山格式，在几何和正则性条件下证明其强收敛性。将该方法应用于黎曼朗之万动力学的几何EM离散化，获得流形上采样的Wasserstein界。

Result: 证明了在适当几何和正则性条件下，黎曼流形上SDE的几何欧拉-丸山格式具有1/2阶强收敛性。作为应用，通过黎曼朗之万动力学的几何EM离散化获得了流形上采样的Wasserstein界。

Conclusion: 该工作为流形上随机微分方程的数值离散化提供了收敛理论，填补了欧几里得空间与流形设置之间的理论差距，为流形上的生成模型和采样算法提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Low-dimensional structure in real-world data plays an important role in the success of generative models, which motivates diffusion models defined on intrinsic data manifolds. Such models are driven by stochastic differential equations (SDEs) on manifolds, which raises the need for convergence theory of numerical schemes for manifold-valued SDEs. In Euclidean space, the Euler--Maruyama (EM) scheme achieves strong convergence with order $1/2$, but an analogous result for manifold discretizations is less understood in general settings. In this work, we study a geometric version of the EM scheme for SDEs on Riemannian manifolds and prove strong convergence with order $1/2$ under geometric and regularity conditions. As an application, we obtain a Wasserstein bound for sampling on manifolds via the geometric EM discretization of Riemannian Langevin dynamics.

</details>


### [110] [Observationally Informed Adaptive Causal Experimental Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03785)
*Erdun Gao,Liang Zhang,Jake Fawkes,Aoqi Zuo,Wenqin Liu,Haoxuan Li,Mingming Gong,Dino Sejdinovic*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出Active Residual Learning新范式，利用观测模型作为先验，将实验重点从从头学习因果量转移到高效估计校正观测偏差所需的残差，显著提升RCT设计效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RCT是因果推断的金标准但资源稀缺，大规模观测数据仅用于回顾性融合，前瞻性试验设计因偏差担忧而被丢弃。这种"白板"数据获取策略本质上效率低下。

Method: 提出Active Residual Learning范式，利用观测模型作为基础先验，引入R-Design框架。提出R-EPIG（残差期望预测信息增益）准则，直接针对因果估计量，最小化残差不确定性或澄清决策边界。

Result: 理论证明两个关键优势：1）结构效率差距，证明估计平滑残差对比比重构完整结果收敛更快；2）信息效率，量化标准参数获取的冗余性。实验在合成和半合成基准上显示R-Design显著优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 修复有偏模型比从头学习模型更高效，Active Residual Learning为利用观测数据改进前瞻性试验设计提供了新范式，R-Design框架在理论和实验上均表现出优越性。

Abstract: Randomized Controlled Trials (RCTs) represent the gold standard for causal inference yet remain a scarce resource. While large-scale observational data is often available, it is utilized only for retrospective fusion, and remains discarded in prospective trial design due to bias concerns. We argue this "tabula rasa" data acquisition strategy is fundamentally inefficient. In this work, we propose Active Residual Learning, a new paradigm that leverages the observational model as a foundational prior. This approach shifts the experimental focus from learning target causal quantities from scratch to efficiently estimating the residuals required to correct observational bias. To operationalize this, we introduce the R-Design framework. Theoretically, we establish two key advantages: (1) a structural efficiency gap, proving that estimating smooth residual contrasts admits strictly faster convergence rates than reconstructing full outcomes; and (2) information efficiency, where we quantify the redundancy in standard parameter-based acquisition (e.g., BALD), demonstrating that such baselines waste budget on task-irrelevant nuisance uncertainty. We propose R-EPIG (Residual Expected Predictive Information Gain), a unified criterion that directly targets the causal estimand, minimizing residual uncertainty for estimation or clarifying decision boundaries for policy. Experiments on synthetic and semi-synthetic benchmarks demonstrate that R-Design significantly outperforms baselines, confirming that repairing a biased model is far more efficient than learning one from scratch.

</details>


### [111] [Invariance-Based Dynamic Regret Minimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03843)
*Margherita Lazzaretto,Jonas Peters,Niklas Pfister*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: ISD-linUCB算法利用历史数据学习奖励模型中的不变性，在快速变化环境中显著降低遗憾


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有非平稳线性bandit算法通过丢弃或降权历史数据来适应变化，但历史数据可能仍包含部分有用信息。本文旨在利用历史数据中的不变性来提升在线性能。

Method: 提出ISD-linUCB算法，假设奖励模型可分解为平稳和非平稳分量，利用历史数据学习不变性（平稳分量），并在在线阶段利用这些不变性降低问题维度。

Result: 理论和实验表明，利用不变性可以降低问题维度，在快速变化环境中，当有足够历史数据时能显著减少遗憾。

Conclusion: 通过分解奖励模型为平稳和非平稳分量，并利用历史数据学习不变性，可以在非平稳线性bandit中有效提升性能，特别是在快速变化环境中。

Abstract: We consider stochastic non-stationary linear bandits where the linear parameter connecting contexts to the reward changes over time. Existing algorithms in this setting localize the policy by gradually discarding or down-weighting past data, effectively shrinking the time horizon over which learning can occur. However, in many settings historical data may still carry partial information about the reward model. We propose to leverage such data while adapting to changes, by assuming the reward model decomposes into stationary and non-stationary components. Based on this assumption, we introduce ISD-linUCB, an algorithm that uses past data to learn invariances in the reward model and subsequently exploits them to improve online performance. We show both theoretically and empirically that leveraging invariance reduces the problem dimensionality, yielding significant regret improvements in fast-changing environments when sufficient historical data is available.

</details>


### [112] [Beyond Mixtures and Products for Ensemble Aggregation: A Likelihood Perspective on Generalized Means](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04204)
*Raphaël Razafindralambo,Rémy Sun,Frédéric Precioso,Damien Garreau,Pierre-Alexandre Mattei*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该研究通过归一化广义均值框架分析密度聚合方法，证明r∈[0,1]是唯一能确保相对于个体分布系统性改进的区间，为线性和几何池化的可靠性提供了理论依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密度聚合是机器学习中的核心问题，特别是在深度集成中组合预测时。线性池化（概率平均）和几何池化（logit平均）两种常用方法的选择仍是一个开放问题。本研究旨在通过标准评估准则（对数似然）的视角，为密度聚合提供统一的理论框架和最优配置指导。

Method: 研究采用归一化广义均值（order r∈ℝ∪{-∞,+∞}）作为统一聚合形式，通过对数似然这一标准机器学习评估准则进行分析。理论分析确定了不同情况下的最优配置，并通过深度集成在图像和文本分类基准上进行实证验证。

Result: 理论证明显示，r∈[0,1]是唯一能确保相对于个体分布系统性改进的区间，这为线性池化（r=1）和几何池化（r=0）的可靠性和广泛应用提供了理论依据。相反，r∉[0,1]的聚合规则可能无法提供一致的改进，并给出了明确的反例。实证结果与理论发现一致。

Conclusion: 归一化广义均值为密度聚合提供了统一的形式化框架，确定了r∈[0,1]为唯一能确保系统性改进的参数区间。这一发现为深度集成中常用的线性和几何池化方法提供了坚实的理论基础，并指导了实际应用中聚合规则的选择。

Abstract: Density aggregation is a central problem in machine learning, for instance when combining predictions from a Deep Ensemble. The choice of aggregation remains an open question with two commonly proposed approaches being linear pooling (probability averaging) and geometric pooling (logit averaging). In this work, we address this question by studying the normalized generalized mean of order $r \in \mathbb{R} \cup \{-\infty,+\infty\}$ through the lens of log-likelihood, the standard evaluation criterion in machine learning. This provides a unifying aggregation formalism and shows different optimal configurations for different situations. We show that the regime $r \in [0,1]$ is the only range ensuring systematic improvements relative to individual distributions, thereby providing a principled justification for the reliability and widespread practical use of linear ($r=1$) and geometric ($r=0$) pooling. In contrast, we show that aggregation rules with $r \notin [0,1]$ may fail to provide consistent gains with explicit counterexamples. Finally, we corroborate our theoretical findings with empirical evaluations using Deep Ensembles on image and text classification benchmarks.

</details>
